const CAM=[3,2,1,6,5,4,9,8,7], NUM=[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9];
const COL={A_Low:'var(--low)',B_Optimal:'var(--opt)',C_High:'var(--high)'};

function order(){let o=st.cam?CAM.slice():NUM.slice(); if(st.rot)o.reverse(); return o}
function btns(){lay.className=st.cam?'on':''; lay.textContent=st.cam?'Camera view':'Number order';
 rot.className=st.rot?'on':''; raw.className=st.raw?'on':''}
lay.onclick=()=>{st.cam=!st.cam;save();btns();draw()}; rot.onclick=()=>{st.rot=!st.rot;save();btns();draw()};
raw.onclick=()=>{st.raw=!st.raw;save();btns();draw()};
function spark(a){ if(a.length<2)return''; const w=100,h=26,mn=Math.min(...a)-1,mx=Math.max(...a)+1;
 const p=a.map((v,i)=>(i*w/(a.length-1)).toFixed(1)+','+(h-(v-mn)/(mx-mn)*h).toFixed(1)).join(' ');
 return `<svg class="spark" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none"><polyline points="${p}" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/></svg>`}
function draw(){ if(!last)return; const now=Date.now();
 let best=null,bestD=0;
 last.pots.forEach(p=>{const h=hist[p.id]||[]; const old=h.find(x=>now-x.t<=8000); if(old){const d=Math.abs(p.raw-old.raw); if(d>bestD){bestD=d;best=p.id}}});
 if(bestD<80)best=null;
 grid.innerHTML=order().map(n=>{const p=last.pots[n-1]; const c=COL[p.grp]||'var(--ink2)';
  const bad=p.pct<0, v=bad?0:p.pct; let cls='in',txt='in band';
  if(p.q){cls='bad';txt='quarantined (sensor fault)'} else if(bad){cls='bad';txt='sensor out of range'}
  else if(v<p.lo){cls='lo';txt='below band â†’ will water'} else if(v>p.hi){cls='hi';txt='above band'}
  const h=(hist[p.id]||[]).map(x=>st.raw?x.raw:x.pct);
  const big=st.raw?`${p.raw}<small> ADC</small>`:(bad?'â€”':`${v.toFixed(1)}<small>%</small>`);
  return `<div class="tile${best===p.id?' hl':''}">${best===p.id?'<span class="flag">changing</span>':''}
  <div class="id" style="color:${c}">${p.id}</div><div class="grp">${last.germ?'Germination':NAME[p.grp]} Â· band ${p.lo}â€“${p.hi}%</div>
  <div class="pct">${big}</div>
  <div class="meter"><div class="band" style="left:${p.lo}%;width:${p.hi-p.lo}%;background:${c}"></div><div class="val" style="left:calc(${v}% - 1px)"></div></div>
  <div class="st ${cls}">${txt}</div>
  <div style="color:${c}">${spark(h)}</div>
  <div class="meta">raw ${p.raw} Â· ch ${p.ch} Â· 15-min ${p.logged<0?'â€”':p.logged.toFixed(1)+'%'}<br>watered ${p.n}Ã—</div></div>`}).join('');
}
async function tick(){
 try{ const r=await fetch('/api',{cache:'no-store'}); const j=await r.json(); last=j; fails=0; const now=Date.now();
  j.pots.forEach(p=>{const h=hist[p.id]=hist[p.id]||[]; h.push({t:now,pct:p.pct<0?0:p.pct,raw:p.raw}); while(h.length&&now-h[0].t>120000)h.shift()});
  dot.className='dot on'; conn.textContent='live Â· '+(j.age_ms/1000).toFixed(1)+' s old';
  mode.textContent=j.irrigating?'WATERING NOW':(j.germ?'Germination mode (all pots 50â€“60%)':'Treatment bands');
  temp.textContent=j.bmp?('enclosure '+j.temp.toFixed(1)+' Â°C'):'enclosure sensor offline';
  clock.textContent=j.synced?new Date(j.t*1000).toLocaleTimeString():'clock not synced';
  foot.textContent='ESP32 up '+Math.floor(j.up/3600)+' h '+Math.floor(j.up%3600/60)+' min';
  draw();
 }catch(e){ if(++fails>2){dot.className='dot';conn.textContent='no connection â€” are you on the AgriSentry Wi-Fi?'} }
 setTimeout(tick,2000);
}
let dur=2000;
function wdraw(){ durs.innerHTML=[1000,2000,5000,10000].map(d=>`<button class="${d===dur?'on':''}" data-d="${d}">${d/1000} s Â· ~${Math.round(d/1000*18)} mL</button>`).join('');
 wgrid.innerHTML=order().map(n=>`<button data-p="${n}">P-0${n}</button>`).join('') }
durs.onclick=e=>{const d=e.target.dataset.d; if(d){dur=+d; wdraw()}};
wgrid.onclick=async e=>{const n=e.target.dataset.p; if(!n)return;
 if(last&&last.irrigating){wmsg.textContent='Busy: watering in progress, wait a moment.';return}
 if(!confirm(`Water P-0${n} for ${dur/1000} s (about ${Math.round(dur/1000*18)} mL)?\nValve ${n} opens, then the pump runs.`))return;
 wmsg.textContent=`Starting P-0${n}â€¦`;
 try{const r=await fetch(`/water?pot=${n}&ms=${dur}`,{method:'POST'}); const t=await r.text();
  wmsg.textContent=r.ok?`P-0${n}: valve + pump ON for ${dur/1000} s. Check that water comes out at pot P-0${n}.`:`Not started: ${t}`}
 catch(err){wmsg.textContent='No answer from the ESP32.'}};
lay.addEventListener('click',wdraw); rot.addEventListener('click',wdraw);
btns(); wdraw(); tick();
</script></body></html>)HTML";


=== FILE: \Dual_Archi\Dual_Archi_Firmware\src\main.cpp ===

/* ===========================================================================
 * ESP32-S3 Autonomous Baseline Telemetry, Irrigation & Vision Flash Controller
 * Target : Custom PCB (ESP32-S3-WROOM-1-N4R2 / 4MB Flash + 2MB PSRAM)
 * Thesis : Computer Vision-Based Growth Monitoring and Yield Prediction for
 *          Pechay Under Differential Soil Moisture Treatments in a Greenhouse
 * ========================================================================= */

#include <Arduino.h>
#include <Wire.h>
#include <Adafruit_Sensor.h>
#include <Adafruit_BMP3XX.h>
#include <Preferences.h>
#include "esp_task_wdt.h"
#include <WiFi.h>
#include <WebServer.h>
#include "web_dashboard.h"

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * ACTUATOR MASTER SWITCH  <-- the only line to change when the 12V side is ready
 *
 *   false : relays (9 solenoids, pump, fans, light) are NEVER energised.
 *           Irrigation decisions are still computed and reported as
 *           "LOG,ACTUATORS_OFF,..." lines so the logic can be verified.
 *   true  : normal operation -- irrigation, fan and light control drive relays.
 *
 * Rebuild + reflash after changing. The current state is announced on boot as
 * "BOOT,actuators=ENABLED|DISABLED".
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
const bool ACTUATORS_ENABLED = true;

const int NUM_PODS = 9;

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * HARDWARE PIN DEFINITIONS (ESP32-S3 Custom PCB)
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
// CD74HC4067 16-Channel Analog Multiplexer
const int MUX_SIG    = 1;                 // ADC1_CH0 (PCB: io1)
const int MUX_SEL[4] = {2, 3, 4, 5};      // S0->io2, S1->io3, S2->io4, S3->io5
// Pot -> MUX channel. The probes are wired rotated 180 deg against the pot
// numbering used by the camera ROIs and the valves (found 2026-10-16 with
// tools/map_probes.py + a hand-over-pot camera frame + a valve spray test):
// the probe in pot P-0n sits on MUX channel 10-n.
const int POD_MUX_CH[9] = {8, 7, 6, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1, 0};

// Relays (Active-LOW)
const int solenoidPins[NUM_PODS] = {6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14}; // Relays 1-9 (12V Solenoids)
const int PUMP_PIN       = 15;            // Relay 10: 12V Water Pump (PCB: io15)
const int FAN_RELAY_PIN  = 16;            // Relay 11: 5V Pi Cooling Fans (PCB: io16)
const int LIGHT_PIN      = 17;            // Relay 12: Grow / Capture Light Flash (PCB: io17)

// BMP388 I2C Interface
const int BMP_SDA = 41;                   // PCB: io41
const int BMP_SCL = 40;                   // PCB: io40

// Raspberry Pi UART Interface (Hardware UART0)
const int PI_UART_RX = 44;                // PCB: U0RXD <- Pi Pin 8 (TXD)
const int PI_UART_TX = 43;                // PCB: U0TXD -> Pi Pin 10 (RXD)

// UI Peripherals
const int LED_INDICATOR_PIN = 21;         // PCB: io21 (Active-LOW)
const int PROG_BUTTON_PIN   = 38;         // PCB: io38

const bool RELAY_ACTIVE_LOW = true;

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * TREATMENT GROUPS & GERMINATION LOGIC
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
enum TreatmentGroup { GROUP_LOW = 0, GROUP_OPTIMAL = 1, GROUP_HIGH = 2 };

struct PodConfig {
  TreatmentGroup group;
  float lowerBound;
  float upperBound;
};

PodConfig podConfig[NUM_PODS] = {
  {GROUP_LOW,     30.0f, 40.0f},   // P-01
  {GROUP_LOW,     30.0f, 40.0f},   // P-02
  {GROUP_LOW,     30.0f, 40.0f},   // P-03
  {GROUP_OPTIMAL, 50.0f, 60.0f},   // P-04
  {GROUP_OPTIMAL, 50.0f, 60.0f},   // P-05
  {GROUP_OPTIMAL, 50.0f, 60.0f},   // P-06
  {GROUP_HIGH,    70.0f, 80.0f},   // P-07
  {GROUP_HIGH,    70.0f, 80.0f},   // P-08
  {GROUP_HIGH,    70.0f, 80.0f}    // P-09
};

const char* groupName(TreatmentGroup g) {
  switch (g) {
    case GROUP_LOW:     return "A_Low";
    case GROUP_OPTIMAL: return "B_Optimal";
    default:            return "C_High";
  }
}

bool  germinationMode        = true;
const float GERM_LOWER_BOUND = 50.0f;
const float GERM_UPPER_BOUND = 60.0f;

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * SENSOR CALIBRATION & HYDRAULIC CONSTANTS
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
// Shared two-point calibration (oven-dried soil = 2483, saturated = 1120), with
// an offset for two probes that read high (2026-10-28, all 9 probes cleaned and
// held in air): the other seven read 4.1-7.0 % (mean 5.9 %), P-03 read 13.5 %
// and P-05 11.4 %. Their dry AND wet points are shifted by the same count
// (P-03 -103, P-05 -75) so they read like the rest in air; the span is unchanged.
//                        P-01  P-02  P-03  P-04  P-05  P-06  P-07  P-08  P-09
int dryADC[NUM_PODS] = {2483, 2483, 2380, 2483, 2408, 2483, 2483, 2483, 2483};
int wetADC[NUM_PODS] = {1120, 1120, 1017, 1120, 1045, 1120, 1120, 1120, 1120};

const int ADC_FAULT_LOW  = 150;
const int ADC_FAULT_HIGH = 4000;

const float ML_PER_PERCENT     = 3.18f;
const float PUMP_FLOW_ML_PER_S = 18.0f;   // measured 2026-10-12: 90 mL in 5 s, all 9 valves
const float MAX_DOSE_ML        = 100.0f;

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * THERMAL MANAGEMENT (BMP388)
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
Adafruit_BMP3XX bmp;
bool  bmpAvailable      = false;
float currentTemp       = 0.0f;
float currentPressure   = 0.0f;
const float FAN_ON_TEMP  = 35.0f;
const float FAN_OFF_TEMP = 32.0f;

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * TIMING CONSTANTS & LIGHT TRACKING
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
const unsigned long SAMPLE_INTERVAL_MS    = 15UL * 60UL * 1000UL;
const unsigned long SM_REPORT_INTERVAL_MS = 30UL * 60UL * 1000UL;
const unsigned long BMP_POLL_INTERVAL_MS  = 5UL * 1000UL;
const unsigned long STABILIZATION_MS      = 2UL * 60UL * 60UL * 1000UL;
const unsigned long MAX_PULSE_MS          = 30000UL;
const int  ADC_SAMPLES                    = 9;
const int  MAX_CONSECUTIVE_FAULTS         = 3;

Preferences prefs;

float         lastVWC[NUM_PODS];
unsigned long lastIrrigationEpoch[NUM_PODS];
uint32_t      irrigationCount[NUM_PODS];
int           consecutiveFaults[NUM_PODS];
bool          podQuarantined[NUM_PODS];

unsigned long lastSampleMs = 0;
unsigned long lastReportMs = 0;
unsigned long lastBmpMs    = 0;
unsigned long lastLedBlinkMs = 0;
bool          ledState     = false;

unsigned long lightOffAtMs = 0;
bool          lightActive  = false;

unsigned long epochAtBoot = 0;
bool          timeSynced  = false;

// Clock "buddy" for the Pi: once synced, the ESP32 keeps time and can hand it
// back to a Pi that rebooted without internet (GET_TIME). Every 10 min the
// synced time is saved to flash, so after a total power loss the last saved
// value says roughly when this board went down.
unsigned long prevAliveEpoch = 0;     // last saved "alive" time from BEFORE this boot
unsigned long lastAliveSaveMs = 0;
const unsigned long ALIVE_SAVE_MS = 10UL * 60UL * 1000UL;
volatile bool irrigating  = false;

void drainPiLink();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * LIVE WEB PAGE (added 2026-10-28)
 *
 * The ESP32 runs its own Wi-Fi network; join it and open http://192.168.4.1
 * for fresh probe readings every 2 s (for sorting probes, checking pots, and
 * showing the panel). The only action on the page is a watering TEST for one
 * pot (valve + pump, max 10 s), which runs the existing MANUAL_WATER command,
 * so the Pi logs it as ACK,MANUAL_WATER. Nothing else can be switched.
 *
 * The web readings are separate from the control loop: they do not touch
 * lastVWC, the fault counters or quarantine, so the 15-min irrigation logic
 * and the SM lines sent to the Pi behave exactly as before. Probes are on
 * ADC1 (io1), which keeps working while Wi-Fi is on.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
const char* AP_SSID = "AgriSentry";
const char* AP_PASS = "agrisentry";        // network key only; the page is read-only
const unsigned long LIVE_READ_MS = 2000UL;
WebServer     web(80);
bool          webUp = false;
int           liveRaw[NUM_PODS];
float         livePct[NUM_PODS];
unsigned long liveAtMs = 0;

uint64_t millis64() {
  static uint32_t low32 = 0, high32 = 0;
  uint32_t now32 = millis();
  if (now32 < low32) high32++;
  low32 = now32;
  return ((uint64_t)high32 << 32) | low32;
}

unsigned long nowEpoch() { 
  return epochAtBoot + (unsigned long)(millis64() / 1000ULL); 
}

inline void relayOn(int pin) {
  if (!ACTUATORS_ENABLED) return;
  digitalWrite(pin, RELAY_ACTIVE_LOW ? LOW : HIGH);
}
inline void relayOff(int pin) { 
  digitalWrite(pin, RELAY_ACTIVE_LOW ? HIGH : LOW); 
}

void emit(const String& line) {
  Serial.println(line);
}

void reportError(const char* code, const String& detail) {
  emit("ERR," + String(nowEpoch()) + "," + code + "," + detail);
}

void checkEnclosureThermal() {
  if (!bmpAvailable) return;
  if (!bmp.performReading()) {
    reportError("BMP_READ_FAIL", "I2C read failure");
    return;
  }
  currentTemp = bmp.temperature;
  currentPressure = bmp.pressure / 100.0F;

  if (currentTemp >= FAN_ON_TEMP) {
    relayOn(FAN_RELAY_PIN);
  } else if (currentTemp <= FAN_OFF_TEMP) {
    relayOff(FAN_RELAY_PIN);
  }
}

int readMuxRaw(int channel) {
  for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    digitalWrite(MUX_SEL[i], (channel >> i) & 0x01);
  }
  delayMicroseconds(80);
  return analogRead(MUX_SIG);
}

int readMuxMedian(int channel) {
  int s[ADC_SAMPLES];
  for (int i = 0; i < ADC_SAMPLES; i++) {
    s[i] = readMuxRaw(channel);
    delay(2);
  }
  for (int i = 1; i < ADC_SAMPLES; i++) {
    int k = s[i], j = i - 1;
    while (j >= 0 && s[j] > k) { s[j + 1] = s[j]; j--; }
    s[j + 1] = k;
  }
  return s[ADC_SAMPLES / 2];
}

float readMoisturePercent(int pod) {
  int raw = readMuxMedian(POD_MUX_CH[pod]);

  if (raw < ADC_FAULT_LOW || raw > ADC_FAULT_HIGH) {
    consecutiveFaults[pod]++;
    reportError("SENSOR_RANGE", "P-0" + String(pod + 1) + ";raw=" + String(raw));
    if (consecutiveFaults[pod] >= MAX_CONSECUTIVE_FAULTS && !podQuarantined[pod]) {
      podQuarantined[pod] = true;
      lastVWC[pod] = -1.0f;
      prefs.putBool(("p" + String(pod) + "q").c_str(), true);
      reportError("POD_QUARANTINE", "P-0" + String(pod + 1) + ";irrigation_suspended");
    }
    return -1.0f;
  }
  consecutiveFaults[pod] = 0;

  int span = dryADC[pod] - wetADC[pod];
  if (span == 0) {
    reportError("CAL_INVALID", "P-0" + String(pod + 1) + ";dry==wet");
    return -1.0f;
  }
  float pct = (float)(dryADC[pod] - raw) / (float)span * 100.0f;
  return constrain(pct, 0.0f, 100.0f);
}

// Display-only read: same median + calibration as readMoisturePercent(), but
// no fault counting, no quarantine, no error lines.
void liveRead() {
  for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) {
    int raw = readMuxMedian(POD_MUX_CH[i]);
    liveRaw[i] = raw;
    int span = dryADC[i] - wetADC[i];
    if (raw < ADC_FAULT_LOW || raw > ADC_FAULT_HIGH || span == 0) { livePct[i] = -1.0f; continue; }
    livePct[i] = constrain((float)(dryADC[i] - raw) / (float)span * 100.0f, 0.0f, 100.0f);
  }
  liveAtMs = millis();
}

void activeBand(int pod, float &lower, float &upper) {
  if (germinationMode) { lower = GERM_LOWER_BOUND; upper = GERM_UPPER_BOUND; }
  else                 { lower = podConfig[pod].lowerBound;
                         upper = podConfig[pod].upperBound; }
}

void handleCommand(String cmd);

// POST /water?pot=1..9&ms=500..10000 -- watering test: valve, then pump.
// Replies first, then runs (blocking, like a normal dose); the page polls
// /api and shows "WATERING NOW" meanwhile.
void handleWater() {
  if (web.method() != HTTP_POST) { web.send(405, "text/plain", "POST only"); return; }
  int pot = web.arg("pot").toInt();
  long ms = web.arg("ms").toInt();
  if (pot < 1 || pot > NUM_PODS || ms < 500 || ms > 10000) {
    web.send(400, "text/plain", "pot 1-9, ms 500-10000"); return;
  }
  if (irrigating) { web.send(409, "text/plain", "busy: watering in progress"); return; }
  if (!ACTUATORS_ENABLED) { web.send(503, "text/plain", "actuators disabled in firmware"); return; }
  web.send(200, "text/plain", "ok");
  emit("LOG,WEB_TEST,P-0" + String(pot) + "," + String(ms) + "ms");
  handleCommand("MANUAL_WATER," + String(pot - 1) + "," + String(ms));
}

void handleApi() {
  String j;
  j.reserve(1400);
  j += "{\"up\":" + String(millis() / 1000UL);
  j += ",\"t\":" + String(nowEpoch());
  j += ",\"synced\":" + String(timeSynced ? "true" : "false");
  j += ",\"germ\":" + String(germinationMode ? "true" : "false");
  j += ",\"irrigating\":" + String(irrigating ? "true" : "false");
  j += ",\"bmp\":" + String(bmpAvailable ? "true" : "false");
  j += ",\"temp\":" + String(currentTemp, 1);
  j += ",\"age_ms\":" + String(liveAtMs ? millis() - liveAtMs : 0UL);
  j += ",\"pots\":[";
  for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) {
    float lo, hi; activeBand(i, lo, hi);
    if (i) j += ",";
    j += "{\"id\":\"P-0" + String(i + 1) + "\",\"ch\":" + String(POD_MUX_CH[i]);
    j += ",\"raw\":" + String(liveRaw[i]) + ",\"pct\":" + String(livePct[i], 1);
    j += ",\"logged\":" + String(lastVWC[i], 1);
    j += ",\"lo\":" + String(lo, 0) + ",\"hi\":" + String(hi, 0);
    j += ",\"grp\":\"" + String(groupName(podConfig[i].group)) + "\"";
    j += ",\"n\":" + String(irrigationCount[i]);
    j += ",\"q\":" + String(podQuarantined[i] ? "true" : "false") + "}";
  }
  j += "]}";
  web.sendHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  web.send(200, "application/json", j);
}

void startWeb() {
  WiFi.mode(WIFI_AP);
  if (!WiFi.softAP(AP_SSID, AP_PASS, 6, 0, 4)) { emit("BOOT,web=FAILED"); return; }
  WiFi.setTxPower(WIFI_POWER_8_5dBm);      // greenhouse range is enough; lower current draw
  web.on("/", []() { web.send_P(200, "text/html", DASHBOARD_HTML); });
  web.on("/api", handleApi);
  web.on("/water", handleWater);
  web.onNotFound([]() { web.sendHeader("Location", "/"); web.send(302, "text/plain", ""); });
  web.begin();
  webUp = true;
  emit("BOOT,web=AP " + String(AP_SSID) + " http://" + WiFi.softAPIP().toString());
}

void saveState(int pod) {
  String k = "p" + String(pod);
  prefs.putULong((k + "t").c_str(), lastIrrigationEpoch[pod]);
  prefs.putUInt ((k + "n").c_str(), irrigationCount[pod]);
}

void loadState() {
  germinationMode = prefs.getBool("germ", true);
  for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) {
    String k = "p" + String(i);
    lastIrrigationEpoch[i] = prefs.getULong((k + "t").c_str(), 0);
    irrigationCount[i]     = prefs.getUInt ((k + "n").c_str(), 0);
    consecutiveFaults[i]   = 0;
    podQuarantined[i]      = prefs.getBool((k + "q").c_str(), false);
    lastVWC[i]             = -1.0f;
  }
}

void irrigatePod(int pod, float vwcBefore) {
  float lower, upper;
  activeBand(pod, lower, upper);

  float target  = (lower + upper) / 2.0f;
  float deficit = target - vwcBefore;
  if (deficit <= 0.0f) return;

  float doseMl = deficit * ML_PER_PERCENT;
  if (doseMl > MAX_DOSE_ML) doseMl = MAX_DOSE_ML;

  unsigned long pulseMs = (unsigned long)((doseMl / PUMP_FLOW_ML_PER_S) * 1000.0f);
  if (pulseMs > MAX_PULSE_MS) {
    pulseMs = MAX_PULSE_MS;
    reportError("PULSE_CLAMPED", "P-0" + String(pod + 1));
  }

  if (!ACTUATORS_ENABLED) {
    emit("LOG,ACTUATORS_OFF,P-0" + String(pod + 1) +
         ",would_dose_ml=" + String(doseMl, 1) +
         ",vwc=" + String(vwcBefore, 1));
    return;
  }

  irrigating = true;
  relayOn(solenoidPins[pod]);
  delay(250);
  relayOn(PUMP_PIN);

  unsigned long t0 = millis();
  while (millis() - t0 < pulseMs) {
    drainPiLink();
    delay(20);
  }

  relayOff(PUMP_PIN);
  delay(250);
  relayOff(solenoidPins[pod]);
  irrigating = false;

  irrigationCount[pod]++;
  lastIrrigationEpoch[pod] = nowEpoch();
  saveState(pod);

  emit("EVT," + String(nowEpoch()) +
       ",P-0" + String(pod + 1) +
       "," + (germinationMode ? "GERM_UNIFORM" : groupName(podConfig[pod].group)) +
       "," + String(vwcBefore, 1) +
       "," + String(target, 1) +
       "," + String(doseMl, 1) +
       "," + String(pulseMs) +
       "," + String(irrigationCount[pod]) +
       ",DOSED");
}

void reportSM() {
  String line = "SM," + String(nowEpoch());
  for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) {
    line += "," + (lastVWC[i] < 0 ? String("NaN") : String(lastVWC[i], 1));
  }
  emit(line);
}

void reportThermal() {
  emit("CLIM," + String(nowEpoch()) + "," + String(currentTemp, 1) + "," + String(currentPressure, 1));
}

void handleCommand(String cmd) {
  cmd.trim();
  while (cmd.length() > 0 && ((uint8_t)cmd[0] < 32 || (uint8_t)cmd[0] > 126)) {
    cmd.remove(0, 1);
  }
  cmd.trim();
  if (cmd.length() == 0) return;

  // Actuator commands fail loudly when the master switch is off, instead of
  // ACKing a relay that never moved.
  if (!ACTUATORS_ENABLED &&
      (cmd.startsWith("MANUAL_WATER,") || cmd.startsWith("TEST_RELAY,") ||
       cmd.startsWith("TEST_MULTI,"))) {
    reportError("ACTUATORS_DISABLED", cmd);
    return;
  }

  if (cmd.startsWith("MANUAL_WATER,")) {
    int firstComma = cmd.indexOf(',');
    int secondComma = cmd.indexOf(',', firstComma + 1);
    
    if (secondComma != -1) {
      int idx = cmd.substring(firstComma + 1, secondComma).toInt();
      unsigned long duration = cmd.substring(secondComma + 1).toInt();
      
      if (idx >= 0 && idx < NUM_PODS && duration > 0 && duration <= 15000) {
        irrigating = true;
        relayOn(solenoidPins[idx]);
        delay(250);
        relayOn(PUMP_PIN);
        
        unsigned long t0 = millis();
        while (millis() - t0 < duration) {
          drainPiLink();
          delay(20);
        }
        
        relayOff(PUMP_PIN);
        delay(250);
        relayOff(solenoidPins[idx]);
        irrigating = false;
        
        emit("ACK,MANUAL_WATER,P-0" + String(idx + 1) + "," + String(duration) + "ms");
      } else {
        reportError("INVALID_PARAMS", cmd);
      }
    }
  }
  else if (cmd.startsWith("TIME,")) {
    unsigned long e = strtoul(cmd.substring(5).c_str(), NULL, 10);
    if (e > 1700000000UL) {
      epochAtBoot = e - (millis() / 1000UL);
      timeSynced  = true;
      emit("ACK,TIME," + String(nowEpoch()));
    } else {
      reportError("TIME_INVALID", cmd);
    }
  }
  else if (cmd == "GET_SM") {
    reportSM();
  }
  else if (cmd == "GET_CLIM") {
    reportThermal();
  }
  else if (cmd == "GET_SM_FRESH") {
    if (irrigating) {
      reportError("BUSY", "irrigation_in_progress");
    } else {
      for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) {
        if (!podQuarantined[i]) lastVWC[i] = readMoisturePercent(i);
      }
      reportSM();
    }
  }
  else if (cmd == "CLEAR_FAULTS") {
    for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) {
      podQuarantined[i]    = false;
      consecutiveFaults[i] = 0;
      prefs.putBool(("p" + String(i) + "q").c_str(), false);
    }
    emit("ACK,CLEAR_FAULTS," + String(nowEpoch()));
  }
  else if (cmd == "RESET_STATE") {
    // New planting: wipe everything kept in flash (irrigation counts, last
    // irrigation times, quarantines, germination flag) back to defaults.
    prefs.clear();
    loadState();
    emit("ACK,RESET_STATE," + String(nowEpoch()));
  }
  else if (cmd == "GET_TIME") {
    // CLOCK,<epoch now>,<synced 1/0>,<uptime_s>,<last alive epoch before this boot>
    emit("CLOCK," + String(timeSynced ? nowEpoch() : 0UL) + "," + String(timeSynced ? 1 : 0) +
         "," + String(millis() / 1000UL) + "," + String(prevAliveEpoch));
  }
  else if (cmd == "PING") {
    emit("PONG," + String(nowEpoch()) + "," + String(millis() / 1000UL));
  }
  else if (cmd == "BANDS_ON") {
    germinationMode = false;
    prefs.putBool("germ", false);
    emit("ACK,BANDS_ON," + String(nowEpoch()));
  }
  else if (cmd == "BANDS_OFF") {
    germinationMode = true;
    prefs.putBool("germ", true);
    emit("ACK,BANDS_OFF," + String(nowEpoch()));
  }
  else if (cmd == "LIGHT_ON") {
    lightActive = true;
    lightOffAtMs = 0;
    relayOn(LIGHT_PIN);
    emit("ACK,LIGHT_ON," + String(nowEpoch()));
  }
  else if (cmd == "LIGHT_OFF") {
    lightActive = false;
    lightOffAtMs = 0;
    relayOff(LIGHT_PIN);
    emit("ACK,LIGHT_OFF," + String(nowEpoch()));
  }
  else if (cmd.startsWith("LIGHT_PULSE,")) {
    unsigned long dur = cmd.substring(12).toInt();
    if (dur > 0 && dur <= 300000UL) {
      relayOn(LIGHT_PIN);
      lightActive = true;
      lightOffAtMs = millis() + dur;
      emit("ACK,LIGHT_PULSE," + String(dur));
    } else {
      reportError("INVALID_DURATION", cmd);
    }
  }
  else if (cmd == "STATUS") {
    for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) {
      float lo, hi; activeBand(i, lo, hi);
      emit("STAT," + String(nowEpoch()) +
           ",P-0" + String(i + 1) +
           "," + (germinationMode ? "GERM_UNIFORM" : groupName(podConfig[i].group)) +
           "," + String(lo, 0) + "," + String(hi, 0) +
           "," + (lastVWC[i] < 0 ? String("NaN") : String(lastVWC[i], 1)) +
           "," + String(irrigationCount[i]) +
           "," + String(lastIrrigationEpoch[i]) +
           "," + (podQuarantined[i] ? "QUARANTINED" : "OK") +
           "," + (irrigating ? "IRRIGATING" : "IDLE"));
    }
    reportThermal();
  }
  else if (cmd.startsWith("TEST_RELAY,")) {
    // TEST_RELAY,<idx>[,<ms>]  idx 0-8 solenoids, 9 pump, 10 fans, 11 light.
    // ms defaults to 2000, capped at 10000.
    int c2 = cmd.indexOf(',', 11);
    int idx = (c2 == -1 ? cmd.substring(11) : cmd.substring(11, c2)).toInt();
    unsigned long ms = (c2 == -1) ? 2000UL : (unsigned long)cmd.substring(c2 + 1).toInt();
    if (ms == 0 || ms > 10000UL) ms = 2000UL;

    int pin = -1;
    String name;
    if (idx >= 0 && idx < NUM_PODS) { pin = solenoidPins[idx]; name = "P-0" + String(idx + 1); }
    else if (idx == 9)              { pin = PUMP_PIN;          name = "PUMP"; }
    else if (idx == 10)             { pin = FAN_RELAY_PIN;     name = "FANS"; }
    else if (idx == 11)             { pin = LIGHT_PIN;         name = "LIGHT"; }

    if (pin < 0) {
      reportError("INVALID_RELAY_INDEX", String(idx));
    } else {
      relayOn(pin);
      unsigned long t0 = millis();
      while (millis() - t0 < ms) { drainPiLink(); delay(20); }
      relayOff(pin);
      emit("ACK,TEST_RELAY," + name + "," + String(ms));
    }
  }
  else if (cmd.startsWith("TEST_MULTI,")) {
    // TEST_MULTI,<ms>,<idx>,<idx>,...  several relays ON together (load test).
    // idx as TEST_RELAY: 0-8 solenoids, 9 pump, 10 fans, 11 light.
    // Switched on in ascending idx order 250 ms apart (valves before the pump),
    // held for ms (max 10000), then off in reverse order (pump first).
    int RELAY_PINS[12];
    for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) RELAY_PINS[i] = solenoidPins[i];
    RELAY_PINS[9] = PUMP_PIN; RELAY_PINS[10] = FAN_RELAY_PIN; RELAY_PINS[11] = LIGHT_PIN;
    bool want[12] = {false};
    int c = cmd.indexOf(',');
    int c2 = cmd.indexOf(',', c + 1);
    unsigned long ms = (unsigned long)cmd.substring(c + 1, c2 == -1 ? cmd.length() : c2).toInt();
    if (ms == 0 || ms > 10000UL) ms = 2000UL;
    int count = 0;
    bool bad = (c2 == -1);
    while (c2 != -1) {
      int next = cmd.indexOf(',', c2 + 1);
      int idx = cmd.substring(c2 + 1, next == -1 ? cmd.length() : next).toInt();
      if (idx < 0 || idx > 11) bad = true;
      else if (!want[idx]) { want[idx] = true; count++; }
      c2 = next;
    }
    if (bad || count == 0) {
      reportError("INVALID_RELAY_LIST", cmd);
    } else {
      String names;
      for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
        if (!want[i]) continue;
        relayOn(RELAY_PINS[i]);
        names += (names.length() ? "+" : "") + String(i);
        delay(250);
      }
      unsigned long t0 = millis();
      while (millis() - t0 < ms) { drainPiLink(); delay(20); }
      for (int i = 11; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (!want[i]) continue;
        relayOff(RELAY_PINS[i]);
        delay(100);
      }
      emit("ACK,TEST_MULTI," + names + "," + String(ms));
    }
  }
  else if (cmd == "GET_RAW") {
    // Raw median ADC per pot, no calibration. For bench checks: a disconnected
    // probe reads very differently from one sitting in soil.
    String line = "RAW," + String(nowEpoch());
    for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) line += "," + String(readMuxMedian(POD_MUX_CH[i]));
    emit(line);
  }
  else if (cmd == "INFO") {
    emit(String("INFO,actuators=") + (ACTUATORS_ENABLED ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED") +
         ",bmp388=" + (bmpAvailable ? "ONLINE" : "OFFLINE") +
         ",germination_mode=" + (germinationMode ? "ON" : "OFF") +
         ",time_synced=" + (timeSynced ? "YES" : "NO") +
         ",uptime_s=" + String(millis() / 1000UL));
  }
  else if (cmd == "REBOOT") {
    emit("ACK,REBOOT," + String(nowEpoch()));
    delay(100);
    esp_restart();
  }
  else {
    reportError("UNKNOWN_CMD", cmd);
  }
}

#define CMD_QUEUE_DEPTH 4
String cmdQueue[CMD_QUEUE_DEPTH];
int    cmdQueueLen = 0;

void queueCommand(const String& c) {
  if (c.length() == 0) return;
  if (cmdQueueLen < CMD_QUEUE_DEPTH) cmdQueue[cmdQueueLen++] = c;
}

void drainPiLink() {
  static String buf;
  while (Serial.available()) {
    char c = (char)Serial.read();
    if (c == '\n') { queueCommand(buf); buf = ""; }
    else if (c != '\r' && buf.length() < 96) { buf += c; }
  }
}

void pollPiLink() {
  drainPiLink();
  for (int i = 0; i < cmdQueueLen; i++) handleCommand(cmdQueue[i]);
  cmdQueueLen = 0;
}

void setup() {
  // Bind hardware UART0: RX = GPIO 44, TX = GPIO 43
  Serial.begin(115200, SERIAL_8N1, PI_UART_RX, PI_UART_TX);
  delay(200);

  // Status LED (Active-LOW: start OFF)
  pinMode(LED_INDICATOR_PIN, OUTPUT);
  digitalWrite(LED_INDICATOR_PIN, HIGH);
  pinMode(PROG_BUTTON_PIN, INPUT_PULLUP);

  // Initialize Relays OFF (Active-LOW: HIGH = OFF)
  for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) {
    digitalWrite(solenoidPins[i], HIGH);
    pinMode(solenoidPins[i], OUTPUT);
  }
  
  digitalWrite(PUMP_PIN, HIGH);
  pinMode(PUMP_PIN, OUTPUT);

  digitalWrite(FAN_RELAY_PIN, HIGH);
  pinMode(FAN_RELAY_PIN, OUTPUT);

  digitalWrite(LIGHT_PIN, HIGH);
  pinMode(LIGHT_PIN, OUTPUT);

  // Multiplexer Address Select Lines
  for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    pinMode(MUX_SEL[i], OUTPUT);
    digitalWrite(MUX_SEL[i], LOW);
  }

  analogReadResolution(12);
  pinMode(MUX_SIG, INPUT);
  analogSetPinAttenuation(MUX_SIG, ADC_11db);

  // Initialize I2C with bus timeout protection to prevent locking the CPU
  Wire.begin(BMP_SDA, BMP_SCL);
  Wire.setTimeOut(25);
  if (bmp.begin_I2C(0x76, &Wire)) {
    bmpAvailable = true;
    bmp.setTemperatureOversampling(BMP3_OVERSAMPLING_8X);
    bmp.setPressureOversampling(BMP3_OVERSAMPLING_4X);
    bmp.setIIRFilterCoeff(BMP3_IIR_FILTER_COEFF_3);
    bmp.setOutputDataRate(BMP3_ODR_50_HZ);
  } else {
    bmpAvailable = false;
  }

  prefs.begin("irrigation", false);
  loadState();
  prevAliveEpoch = prefs.getULong("alive", 0);

  // Startup announcements to Pi
  emit("BOOT," + String(millis()) + ",ESP32S3_PCB_IRRIGATION,v6-web,relays=12");
  emit(String("BOOT,bmp388=") + (bmpAvailable ? "ONLINE" : "OFFLINE"));
  emit(String("BOOT,germination_mode=") + (germinationMode ? "ON" : "OFF"));
  emit(String("BOOT,actuators=") + (ACTUATORS_ENABLED ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED"));
  emit("BOOT,awaiting TIME sync from Pi");

  liveRead();
  startWeb();

  // Indicate successful setup complete (Turn LED ON)
  digitalWrite(LED_INDICATOR_PIN, LOW);
}

void loop() {
  pollPiLink();

  unsigned long now = millis();

  // Heartbeat LED: Toggles every 1 second to confirm the loop is running
  if (now - lastLedBlinkMs >= 1000UL) {
    lastLedBlinkMs = now;
    ledState = !ledState;
    digitalWrite(LED_INDICATOR_PIN, ledState ? LOW : HIGH);
  }

  // Live web page: serve requests, refresh the display-only readings
  if (webUp) {
    web.handleClient();
    if (!irrigating && now - liveAtMs >= LIVE_READ_MS) liveRead();
  }

  // Auto-shutoff for camera light flash
  if (lightActive && lightOffAtMs > 0 && now >= lightOffAtMs) {
    relayOff(LIGHT_PIN);
    lightActive = false;
    lightOffAtMs = 0;
  }

  // 1. Enclosure Temperature Regulation (every 5 seconds)
  if (now - lastBmpMs >= BMP_POLL_INTERVAL_MS || lastBmpMs == 0) {
    lastBmpMs = now;
    checkEnclosureThermal();
  }

  // 2. Closed-Loop Irrigation Execution (every 15 minutes)
  if (now - lastSampleMs >= SAMPLE_INTERVAL_MS || lastSampleMs == 0) {
    lastSampleMs = now;

    for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PODS; i++) {
      if (podQuarantined[i]) continue;

      float vwc = readMoisturePercent(i);
      lastVWC[i] = vwc;
      if (vwc < 0) continue;

      if (!timeSynced) continue;

      unsigned long sinceLast = nowEpoch() - lastIrrigationEpoch[i];
      if (lastIrrigationEpoch[i] != 0 &&
          sinceLast < (STABILIZATION_MS / 1000UL)) continue;

      float lo, hi; activeBand(i, lo, hi);

      if (vwc < lo) {
        irrigatePod(i, vwc);
      } else if (vwc > hi) {
        reportError("ABOVE_BAND", "P-0" + String(i + 1) + ";vwc=" + String(vwc, 1));
      }

      delay(200);
    }
  }

  // Alive stamp for power-loss forensics (only meaningful once time-synced)
  if (timeSynced && (lastAliveSaveMs == 0 || now - lastAliveSaveMs >= ALIVE_SAVE_MS)) {
    lastAliveSaveMs = now;
    prefs.putULong("alive", nowEpoch());
  }

  // 3. Periodic SM Telemetry Report to Pi (every 30 minutes)
  if (now - lastReportMs >= SM_REPORT_INTERVAL_MS) {
    lastReportMs = now;
    reportSM();
  }

  delay(20);
}

=== FILE: \Dual_Archi\tools\actuator_test.py ===

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""actuator_test.py -- interactive relay / actuator tester.

Pick a relay, pick how long, watch it switch. Every action needs a keypress;
nothing runs on its own.

  Relays:  1-9 solenoids P-01..P-09 | 10 pump | 11 fans | 12 light
  Water:   w <pot> <ms>  opens that pot's solenoid THEN the pump (real dose path)
  Multi:   m 1,2,10 <ms> several relays ON together (load / power-supply test)
  Cycle:   all           each relay in turn, Enter between each
  Quit:    q

Needs firmware built with ACTUATORS_ENABLED = true (top of main.cpp), then
./flash_esp.sh. With it false the ESP32 replies ERR,...,ACTUATORS_DISABLED and
nothing switches.

SAFETY: running the pump alone (10) pushes against closed valves. Keep it short,
or use "w" which opens a valve first.

After every action the ESP32's uptime is checked. If it went DOWN, the board
rebooted during the switching: a power problem (supply sag / inductive spike),
not a software one.
"""
import os
import sys
import time

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from esp_link import EspLink, info_dict, stop_daemon  # noqa: E402

NAMES = [f"Solenoid P-0{i}" for i in range(1, 10)] + ["Pump", "Fans", "Light"]
MAX_MS = 10000


def menu():
    print("\n  #   relay")
    for i, n in enumerate(NAMES, 1):
        print(f"  {i:<3} {n}")
    print("  w <pot 1-9> <ms>   water test (valve + pump)")
    print("  m 1,2,10 <ms>      several relays ON at the same time, e.g. P-01 + P-02 + pump")
    print("  all                cycle every relay (Enter between each)")
    print("  q                  quit")


def uptime(esp):
    try:
        return int(info_dict(esp.ask("INFO", "INFO", 3)).get("uptime_s", -1))
    except ValueError:
        return -1


def watched(esp, cmd, expect, timeout):
    """Send an actuator command and report whether the ESP32 survived it."""
    before = uptime(esp)
    r = esp.ask(cmd, expect, timeout)
    print(f"  <- {r}" if r else "  <- no reply")
    time.sleep(0.3)
    after = uptime(esp)
    if after < 0:
        print("  !! ESP32 not answering after the test -- it may have crashed or browned out")
    elif before >= 0 and after < before:
        print(f"  !! ESP32 REBOOTED during the test (uptime {before}s -> {after}s).\n"
              "     Power problem: the 12V/5V supply sagged or an inductive spike reached the\n"
              "     ESP32. Check flyback diodes, supply current rating, and grounding.")
    else:
        print(f"  ok: ESP32 stayed up (uptime {after}s)")
    return bool(r) and r.startswith("ACK")


def pulse(esp, idx, ms):
    print(f"  -> {NAMES[idx]} ON for {ms} ms ...", flush=True)
    return watched(esp, f"TEST_RELAY,{idx},{ms}", "ACK,TEST_RELAY", ms / 1000 + 3)


def main():
    stop_daemon()
    with EspLink() as esp:
        esp.sync_time()
        info = info_dict(esp.ask("INFO", "INFO", 3))
        state = info.get("actuators", "UNKNOWN (old firmware?)")
        print(f"\nESP32 actuators: {state}")
        if state != "ENABLED":
            print("  Relays will NOT switch. To enable: set ACTUATORS_ENABLED = true at the top of\n"
                  "  Dual_Archi_Firmware/src/main.cpp, then run ./flash_esp.sh on the Mac.")
        menu()
        while True:
            try:
                cmd = input("\nactuator> ").strip().lower()
            except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
                print()
                break
            if cmd in ("q", "quit", "exit"):
                break
            if cmd in ("", "?", "h", "help"):
                menu()
                continue
            parts = cmd.split()
            try:
                if parts[0] == "all":
                    ms = int(parts[1]) if len(parts) > 1 else 1000
                    for idx in range(len(NAMES)):
                        input(f"  Enter to fire {NAMES[idx]} ({ms} ms), Ctrl-C to stop ")
                        pulse(esp, idx, min(ms, MAX_MS))
                elif parts[0] == "w":
                    pot, ms = int(parts[1]), int(parts[2]) if len(parts) > 2 else 2000
                    if not 1 <= pot <= 9:
                        raise ValueError
                    ms = min(ms, 15000)
                    print(f"  -> water P-0{pot}: valve, then pump, {ms} ms")
                    watched(esp, f"MANUAL_WATER,{pot - 1},{ms}", "ACK,MANUAL_WATER", ms / 1000 + 4)
                elif parts[0] == "m":
                    nums = sorted({int(n) for n in parts[1].split(",")})
                    if not nums or not all(1 <= n <= len(NAMES) for n in nums):
                        raise ValueError
                    ms = max(50, min(int(parts[2]) if len(parts) > 2 else 3000, MAX_MS))
                    print("  -> ON together for %d ms: %s" % (ms, ", ".join(NAMES[n - 1] for n in nums)))
                    if 10 in nums and not any(n <= 9 for n in nums):
                        print("     (pump with no valve open -- keep it short)")
                    idxs = ",".join(str(n - 1) for n in nums)
                    watched(esp, f"TEST_MULTI,{ms},{idxs}", "ACK,TEST_MULTI",
                            ms / 1000 + 0.25 * len(nums) + 4)
                else:
                    idx = int(parts[0]) - 1
                    if not 0 <= idx < len(NAMES):
                        raise ValueError
                    ms = int(parts[1]) if len(parts) > 1 else int(
                        input(f"  how long (ms, max {MAX_MS}) [1000]: ") or 1000)
                    pulse(esp, idx, max(50, min(ms, MAX_MS)))
            except (ValueError, IndexError):
                print("  ? try: 3   |   3 1500   |   w 4 2000   |   m 1,2,10 3000   |   all   |   q")
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                print("\n  stopped")
    print("done. pi-link is still stopped; start the system with tools/agrisentry.sh start")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: \Dual_Archi\tools\agrisentry.sh ===

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# agrisentry.sh -- start / stop / check the AgriSentry system on the Pi.
#
#   agrisentry.sh start       start + enable pi-link (ESP32 logger) and the 12:00 GPR cron job
#   agrisentry.sh stop        stop + disable both (testing mode)
#   agrisentry.sh status      what's running, today's day number, CSV row counts, last log lines
#   agrisentry.sh reset-data  archive all data CSVs/logs and start empty ones (asks first)
#   agrisentry.sh new-run YYYY-MM-DD
#                             NEW PLANTING: archive the whole previous run (CSVs, logs,
#                             frames, field log) to ~/archive/, set the sowing date (= D1),
#                             reset the ESP32 (counters cleared, germination mode ON:
#                             all pots 50-60 %), and turn logging back on
#   agrisentry.sh bands-on    end germination mode: start the low/optimal/high treatments
#   agrisentry.sh ups-test    start ONLY the logger (no noon capture) and mark the start
#                             of a UPS runtime test; the result is written to
#                             ~/agrisentry_data/power_log.csv after power comes back
#
# From the Mac:  ./system.sh start|stop|status
set -uo pipefail

DUAL="$HOME/THESIS/Dual_Archi"
DATA="$HOME/agrisentry_data"
# 12:00, with retries every 10 min until 12:50 in case the Pi was rebooting or the camera
# was busy. The duplicate-day guard makes every run after the first successful one a no-op.
CRON_LINE="0,10,20,30,40,50 12 * * * /usr/bin/python3 $DUAL/capture_gpr.py >> $DUAL/cron.log 2>&1"
# Hourly light reading from the camera (tools/light_log.py). Skips 12:xx so it never
# fights the noon capture for the camera.
LIGHT_LINE="30 6-11,13-18 * * * /usr/bin/python3 $DUAL/tools/light_log.py >> $DUAL/light_cron.log 2>&1"

cron_set() {   # cron_set on|off
  local cur; cur=$(crontab -l 2>/dev/null | grep -v -e 'capture_gpr.py' -e 'light_log.py')
  if [[ $1 == on ]]; then printf '%s\n%s\n%s\n' "$cur" "$CRON_LINE" "$LIGHT_LINE" | crontab -
  else printf '%s\n# %s\n# %s\n' "$cur" "$CRON_LINE" "$LIGHT_LINE" | crontab -; fi
}

day_info() {
  python3 - "$DUAL/sowing_date.txt" <<'EOF'
import sys, datetime as dt
s = open(sys.argv[1]).read().strip()
d = (dt.date.today() - dt.date.fromisoformat(s)).days + 1
print(f"sowing date {s} -> today is D={d} (capture_gpr numbering, D=1 on sowing day)")
EOF
}

rows() { [[ -f $1 ]] && echo $(( $(wc -l < "$1") - 1 )) || echo "missing"; }

status() {
  echo "pi-link service : $(systemctl is-active pi-link) ($(systemctl is-enabled pi-link 2>/dev/null))"
  if crontab -l 2>/dev/null | grep -q '^[^#].*capture_gpr.py'; then
    echo "12:00 GPR cron  : ON"
  else
    echo "12:00 GPR cron  : OFF"
  fi
  if [[ $(timedatectl show -p NTPSynchronized --value) == yes ]]; then
    echo "clock           : OK, internet-synced ($(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M'))"
  else
    echo "clock           : NOT SYNCED since boot ($(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')) -- rebooted offline?"
    echo "                  connect the hotspot for a minute so the time corrects"
  fi
  echo "$(day_info)"
  echo "data rows       : growth_dataset=$(rows "$DUAL/growth_dataset.csv")" \
       " sm_log=$(rows "$DATA/sm_log.csv") irrigation=$(rows "$DATA/irrigation_log.csv")" \
       " errors=$(rows "$DATA/error_log.csv") above-band notices=$(rows "$DATA/band_log.csv")"
  if [[ -f $DATA/sm_log.csv ]] && (( $(wc -l < "$DATA/sm_log.csv") > 1 )); then
    python3 - "$DATA/sm_log.csv" <<'EOF'
import csv, sys
rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(sys.argv[1])))
r = rows[-1]
band = {1: "30-40", 2: "30-40", 3: "30-40", 4: "50-60", 5: "50-60", 6: "50-60",
        7: "70-80", 8: "70-80", 9: "70-80"}
print(f"latest moisture : {r['wall_time'].replace('T', ' ')[:16]}  (logged every 30 min)")
for i in range(1, 10):
    v = r[f"P-0{i}"]
    lo, hi = (float(x) for x in band[i].split("-"))
    note = "" if v == "NaN" else ("  below band -> will water" if float(v) < lo else
                                   "  above band (no water)" if float(v) > hi else "  in band")
    print(f"   P-0{i}  {v:>6}%   band {band[i]}%{note}")
EOF
  fi
  if [[ -f $DATA/ups_test_start ]]; then
    echo "UPS test        : RUNNING since $(date -d @"$(cat "$DATA/ups_test_start")" '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')"
  fi
  if [[ -f $DATA/power_log.csv ]]; then
    echo "--- power events (power_log.csv)"
    tail -n 3 "$DATA/power_log.csv" | cut -d, -f2,3,4,5,9
  fi
  if [[ -f $DUAL/pi_link.log ]]; then
    echo "--- last pi_link.log lines (above-band notices hidden)"
    grep -v "ABOVE_BAND" "$DUAL/pi_link.log" | tail -n 6
  fi
}

case "${1:-status}" in
  start)
    if [[ -f $DATA/ups_test_start ]]; then
      echo "Ending the UPS test that started $(date -d @"$(cat "$DATA/ups_test_start")" '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') (no power loss recorded for it)"
      rm -f "$DATA/ups_test_start"
    fi
    sudo -n systemctl enable --now pi-link
    cron_set on
    sleep 4
    if grep -q "ACK,TIME" <(tail -n 20 "$DUAL/pi_link.log" 2>/dev/null); then
      echo "ESP32 link OK (time sync acknowledged)"
    else
      echo "WARNING: no ACK,TIME from the ESP32 yet -- check pi_link.log"
    fi
    status ;;
  stop)
    sudo -n systemctl disable --now pi-link
    cron_set off
    status ;;
  status)
    status ;;
  ups-test)
    if [[ $(timedatectl show -p NTPSynchronized --value) != yes ]]; then
      echo "Pi clock is not internet-synced -- connect the hotspot first so the start time is right"; exit 1
    fi
    date +%s > "$DATA/ups_test_start"
    sudo -n systemctl enable --now pi-link
    sleep 4
    echo "UPS test started $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M'). Logger running (noon capture NOT enabled)."
    echo "Unplug mains now and leave it. After power returns and the Pi boots, run:"
    echo "  ./system.sh status      (or: cat ~/agrisentry_data/power_log.csv)"
    status ;;
  reset-data)
    A="$DATA/archive_$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S)"
    read -r -p "Archive all current CSVs/logs to $A and start fresh? [y/N] " ok
    [[ $ok == [yY] ]] || { echo "cancelled"; exit 1; }
    was=$(systemctl is-active pi-link)
    sudo -n systemctl stop pi-link
    mkdir -p "$A"
    mv "$DATA"/*.csv "$DATA"/sm_snapshot.json "$DUAL"/growth_dataset.csv "$DUAL"/cron.log "$A"/ 2>/dev/null
    [[ -f $DUAL/pi_link.log ]] && sudo -n mv "$DUAL/pi_link.log" "$A/" && sudo -n chown "$USER" "$A/pi_link.log"
    python3 "$DUAL/tools/init_csvs.py"
    [[ $was == active ]] && sudo -n systemctl start pi-link
    echo "archived to $A"; status ;;
  new-run)
    NEW=${2:-}
    if ! [[ $NEW =~ ^20[0-9]{2}-[01][0-9]-[0-3][0-9]$ ]] || ! date -d "$NEW" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      echo "usage: $0 new-run YYYY-MM-DD   (the sowing date, which becomes D1)"; exit 1
    fi
    OLD=$(cat "$DUAL/sowing_date.txt" 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)
    A="$HOME/archive/run_sown_${OLD}_archived_$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S)"
    [[ -e $A ]] && { echo "$A already exists -- wait a second and run again"; exit 1; }
    echo "This will:"
    echo "  1. move ALL data of the run sown $OLD (CSVs, logs, frames, field log) to"
    echo "     $A   (nothing is deleted)"
    echo "  2. set the sowing date to $NEW (D1). Code and pot_rois.json stay as they are."
    echo "  3. reset the ESP32: watering counters cleared, germination mode ON (all pots 50-60 %)"
    echo "  4. turn the logger, the 12:00 capture and the hourly light log ON"
    read -r -p "Go ahead? [y/N] " ok
    [[ $ok == [yY] ]] || { echo "cancelled"; exit 1; }
    sudo -n systemctl stop pi-link
    cron_set off
    mkdir -p "$A/agrisentry_data" "$A/thesis"
    shopt -s dotglob nullglob
    for f in "$DATA"/*; do mv "$f" "$A/agrisentry_data/"; done
    for f in "$DUAL"/growth_dataset*.csv "$DUAL"/exposure_log*.csv "$DUAL"/field_log*.csv \
             "$DUAL"/cell_gpr* "$DUAL"/light_log*.csv "$DUAL"/harvest_weights*.csv \
             "$DUAL"/cron.log "$DUAL"/light_cron.log; do
      [[ -e $f ]] && mv "$f" "$A/thesis/"
    done
    shopt -u dotglob nullglob
    [[ -f $DUAL/pi_link.log ]] && sudo -n mv "$DUAL/pi_link.log" "$A/thesis/" && sudo -n chown "$USER" "$A/thesis/pi_link.log"
    cp "$DUAL"/pot_rois*.json "$DUAL/sowing_date.txt" "$A/thesis/" 2>/dev/null
    echo "$NEW" > "$DUAL/sowing_date.txt"
    python3 "$DUAL/tools/init_csvs.py"
    printf 'date,day,pot_id,note\n%s,,ALL,"New run, sowing date %s = D1. Previous run (sown %s) archived to %s."\n' \
      "$(date +%F)" "$NEW" "$OLD" "$A" > "$DUAL/field_log.csv"
    echo "--- resetting the ESP32 (counters, germination mode ON)"
    if python3 "$DUAL/pi_link.py" --reset-state; then echo "ESP32 reset OK"
    else echo "WARNING: ESP32 did not acknowledge RESET_STATE -- run it again later:"
         echo "  sudo systemctl stop pi-link; python3 ~/thesis/pi_link.py --reset-state; sudo systemctl start pi-link"; fi
    sudo -n systemctl enable --now pi-link
    cron_set on
    echo "archived to $A"
    echo "Next: re-fit the pot circles (make_rois.py) once the camera is mounted, BEFORE 12:00 on $NEW."
    sleep 4; status ;;
  bands-on)
    sudo -n systemctl stop pi-link
    if python3 "$DUAL/pi_link.py" --bands-on; then
      echo "Treatments ON (low 30-40 / optimal 50-60 / high 70-80)"
      printf '%s,%s,ALL,Germination mode OFF -- treatment bands ON\n' "$(date +%F)" \
        "$(( ($(date +%s) - $(date -d "$(cat "$DUAL/sowing_date.txt")" +%s)) / 86400 + 1 ))" >> "$DUAL/field_log.csv"
    else
      echo "WARNING: no ACK from the ESP32 -- bands NOT changed"
    fi
    sudo -n systemctl start pi-link
    status ;;
  *)
    echo "usage: $0 start|stop|status|reset-data|new-run YYYY-MM-DD|bands-on|ups-test"; exit 1 ;;
esac


=== FILE: \Dual_Archi\tools\bench_check.py ===

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""bench_check.py -- one-shot health check of the whole rig. Writes NO CSV data.

  1. ESP32 link        PING / PONG, firmware INFO
  2. BMP388            enclosure temperature / pressure
  3. Soil moisture     raw ADC + calibrated % per pot, with a verdict
  4. Relays            (only with --relays) clicks each relay briefly
  5. Camera            real capture through capture_gpr.py's own ROI + HSV
                       masking (--no-write --no-sm), saved as an overlay PNG

The pi-link service is stopped and LEFT stopped (testing mode). Start the
system again with tools/agrisentry.sh start.

Usually run from the Mac via ./bench_test.sh, which also copies the overlay
image back to the laptop and opens it.
"""
import argparse
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import time

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from esp_link import EspLink, info_dict, stop_daemon  # noqa: E402

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
DUAL = os.path.dirname(HERE)

# Must match the firmware (main.cpp)
ADC_FAULT_LOW, ADC_FAULT_HIGH = 150, 4000
DRY_ADC, WET_ADC = 2483, 1120
RELAY_NAMES = [f"P-0{i}" for i in range(1, 10)] + ["PUMP", "FANS", "LIGHT"]

results = []


def check(name, ok, detail=""):
    results.append((name, ok))
    mark = "PASS" if ok else ("WARN" if ok is None else "FAIL")
    print(f"  [{mark}] {name}" + (f" -- {detail}" if detail else ""))


def sensor_verdict(raw):
    if raw < ADC_FAULT_LOW:
        return False, "below fault floor: disconnected / shorted?"
    if raw > ADC_FAULT_HIGH:
        return False, "above fault ceiling: open circuit / no power?"
    if raw < WET_ADC - 150:
        return None, "wetter than wet calibration (probe in water, or floating input?)"
    if raw > DRY_ADC + 150:
        return None, "drier than dry calibration (probe in air?)"
    return True, "in calibrated range"


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="AgriSentry bench check")
    ap.add_argument("--relays", action="store_true",
                    help="also click every relay (needs ACTUATORS_ENABLED=true firmware)")
    ap.add_argument("--relay-ms", type=int, default=500)
    ap.add_argument("--no-camera", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="/tmp/agrisentry_bench",
                    help="where the overlay image goes")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    os.makedirs(args.out, exist_ok=True)

    stop_daemon()

    print("\n== 1. ESP32 link")
    try:
        esp = EspLink()
    except Exception as exc:
        check("serial port", False, str(exc))
        return finish()
    with esp:
        pongs = sum(1 for _ in range(5) if (esp.ask("PING", "PONG", 2) or "").startswith("PONG"))
        check("PING/PONG", pongs == 5, f"{pongs}/5 replies")
        esp.sync_time()
        info = info_dict(esp.ask("INFO", "INFO", 3))
        if info:
            print(f"         firmware: {info}")
        else:
            check("INFO", None, "no reply (older firmware? reflash with ./flash_esp.sh)")

        print("\n== 2. BMP388 enclosure sensor")
        if info.get("bmp388") == "ONLINE":
            clim = esp.ask("GET_CLIM", "CLIM", 3)
            p = (clim or "").split(",")
            check("BMP388", len(p) == 4, f"{p[2]} C, {p[3]} hPa" if len(p) == 4 else clim)
        else:
            check("BMP388", False, "OFFLINE (not detected at I2C 0x76 on SDA41/SCL40)")

        print("\n== 3. Soil moisture sensors")
        raw = esp.ask("GET_RAW", "RAW", 8)
        sm = esp.ask("GET_SM_FRESH", "SM", 15)
        if raw and raw.startswith("RAW"):
            raws = [int(v) for v in raw.split(",")[2:]]
            pcts = sm.split(",")[2:] if sm and sm.startswith("SM") else ["?"] * 9
            print("         pot    raw ADC   moisture")
            quarantined = 0
            for i, (r, pct) in enumerate(zip(raws, pcts)):
                ok, why = sensor_verdict(r)
                if pct == "NaN":
                    quarantined += 1
                    ok, why = None, why + ", but pot is QUARANTINED (no % reading, no watering)"
                check(f"P-0{i + 1}   {r:5d}   {pct:>6}%", ok, why)
            if quarantined:
                print(f"         {quarantined} pot(s) quarantined after repeated bad readings. Once the\n"
                      "         probes read sensibly, clear it on the Pi:\n"
                      "             python3 ~/thesis/pi_link.py --clear-faults")
            if len(set(raws)) == 1 or max(raws) - min(raws) < 20:
                print("         note: all pots read nearly the same -- typical of the MUX "
                      "with no probes plugged in")
        else:
            check("GET_RAW", False, raw or "no reply (reflash firmware with ./flash_esp.sh)")

        print("\n== 4. Relays")
        if not args.relays:
            print("  [SKIP] pass --relays to click each relay "
                  "(or use ./actuator_test.sh for one at a time)")
        elif info.get("actuators") != "ENABLED":
            check("relays", False, "firmware has ACTUATORS_ENABLED = false -- nothing will switch")
        else:
            for idx, name in enumerate(RELAY_NAMES):
                r = esp.ask(f"TEST_RELAY,{idx},{args.relay_ms}", "ACK,TEST_RELAY",
                            args.relay_ms / 1000 + 3)
                check(f"relay {name}", bool(r) and r.startswith("ACK"),
                      "listen for the click" if r and r.startswith("ACK") else r)
                time.sleep(0.3)

    print("\n== 5. Camera + ROI masking")
    if args.no_camera:
        print("  [SKIP] --no-camera")
    else:
        png = os.path.join(args.out, "bench_overlay.png")
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(DUAL, "capture_gpr.py"),
                            "--no-write", "--no-sm", "--allow-any-day",
                            "--debug-overlay", png],
                           capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120,
                           env={**os.environ, "LIBCAMERA_LOG_LEVELS": "*:ERROR"})
        print("\n".join("         " + l for l in (r.stdout + r.stderr).strip().splitlines()))
        check("camera capture + overlay", r.returncode == 0 and os.path.exists(png), png)

    return finish()


def finish():
    fails = [n for n, ok in results if ok is False]
    warns = [n for n, ok in results if ok is None]
    print(f"\n== SUMMARY: {len(results) - len(fails) - len(warns)} pass, "
          f"{len(warns)} warn, {len(fails)} fail")
    print("   pi-link service is STOPPED (testing mode). "
          "Start the system with: tools/agrisentry.sh start")
    return 1 if fails else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


=== FILE: \Dual_Archi\tools\esp_link.py ===

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""esp_link.py -- small shared helper for the bench / actuator / system tools.

Talks to the ESP32-S3 over /dev/ttyAMA0 directly. The pi-link daemon must NOT
be running (it holds the port exclusively); stop_daemon() takes care of that.
"""
import subprocess
import sys
import time

import serial

PORT = "/dev/ttyAMA0"
BAUD = 115200


def daemon_active():
    r = subprocess.run(["systemctl", "is-active", "pi-link"],
                       capture_output=True, text=True)
    return r.stdout.strip() == "active"


def stop_daemon():
    """Stop pi-link so we can own the serial port. Returns True if it was running."""
    if not daemon_active():
        return False
    print("[esp] stopping pi-link service (frees the serial port)")
    subprocess.run(["sudo", "-n", "systemctl", "stop", "pi-link"], check=True)
    time.sleep(1)
    return True


class EspLink:
    def __init__(self):
        self.ser = serial.Serial(PORT, BAUD, timeout=0.2, exclusive=True)
        time.sleep(0.2)
        self.ser.reset_input_buffer()

    def close(self):
        self.ser.close()

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *exc):
        self.close()

    def ask(self, cmd, expect, timeout=5.0, echo=False):
        """Send cmd, return the first line starting with any prefix in expect
        (str or tuple), or None on timeout. ERR lines are returned too, so the
        caller can see why something was refused."""
        if isinstance(expect, str):
            expect = (expect,)
        self.ser.reset_input_buffer()
        self.ser.write((cmd + "\n").encode())
        deadline = time.time() + timeout
        while time.time() < deadline:
            line = self.ser.readline().decode(errors="replace").strip()
            if not line:
                continue
            if echo:
                print(f"  < {line}")
            if line.startswith(expect) or line.startswith("ERR,"):
                return line
        return None

    def sync_time(self):
        return self.ask(f"TIME,{int(time.time())}", "ACK,TIME")


def info_dict(line):
    """'INFO,a=1,b=2' -> {'a': '1', 'b': '2'}"""
    out = {}
    for part in (line or "").split(",")[1:]:
        if "=" in part:
            k, v = part.split("=", 1)
            out[k] = v
    return out


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Ad-hoc use:  python3 esp_link.py PING PONG
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        sys.exit("usage: esp_link.py <COMMAND> [EXPECT_PREFIX]")
    stop_daemon()
    with EspLink() as esp:
        print(esp.ask(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "", echo=True))


=== FILE: \Dual_Archi\tools\flash_esp.py ===

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""flash_esp.py <dir> -- flash <dir>/{bootloader,partitions,firmware}.bin to the
ESP32-S3 over UART0, using the Pi's GPIO to drive the strapping pins:

    BCM 23 (physical 16) -> ESP32 EN    (reset)
    BCM 24 (physical 18) -> ESP32 BOOT  (GPIO0)

Then resets into run mode and prints the boot output so you can see the new
firmware come up. pi-link is stopped for the flash and restarted afterwards
only if it was running before.

Usually called by ./flash_esp.sh on the Mac (build -> copy -> flash).
"""
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import time

import RPi.GPIO as GPIO
import serial

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from esp_link import PORT, stop_daemon  # noqa: E402

EN, BOOT = 23, 24
ESPTOOL = os.path.expanduser("~/.local/bin/esptool")


def main():
    d = os.path.expanduser(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "~/fw_main")
    images = [("0x0", "bootloader.bin"), ("0x8000", "partitions.bin"), ("0x10000", "firmware.bin")]
    for _, name in images:
        p = os.path.join(d, name)
        if not os.path.isfile(p):
            sys.exit(f"missing {p}")
        print(f"  {name:16s} {os.path.getsize(p):8d} bytes  {time.ctime(os.path.getmtime(p))}")

    was_running = stop_daemon()

    # Something else (bench/actuator test left open, a serial monitor...) may
    # still hold the port. Find out now, before touching EN/BOOT.
    try:
        serial.Serial(PORT, 115200, exclusive=True).close()
    except serial.SerialException:
        holders = subprocess.run(["ps", "-o", "pid=,args=", "-p",
                                  ",".join(subprocess.run(["fuser", PORT], capture_output=True,
                                                          text=True).stdout.split()) or "0"],
                                 capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
        print(f"\nFLASH ABORTED: {PORT} is in use by another program:\n  {holders or '(unknown)'}\n"
              "Close it first (an open ./actuator_test.sh or ./bench_test.sh? press q / Ctrl-C there),\n"
              "or end it on the Pi with:  kill <pid>")
        sys.exit(1)

    GPIO.setmode(GPIO.BCM)
    GPIO.setwarnings(False)
    GPIO.setup(EN, GPIO.OUT, initial=GPIO.HIGH)
    GPIO.setup(BOOT, GPIO.OUT, initial=GPIO.HIGH)

    print("[1/3] entering ROM bootloader (BOOT low, pulse EN)")
    GPIO.output(BOOT, GPIO.LOW); time.sleep(0.1)
    GPIO.output(EN, GPIO.LOW); time.sleep(0.3)
    GPIO.output(EN, GPIO.HIGH); time.sleep(0.3)

    print("[2/3] flashing")
    cmd = [ESPTOOL, "--chip", "esp32s3", "--port", PORT, "--baud", "115200",
           "--before", "no-reset", "--after", "no-reset",
           "write-flash", "--flash-size", "4MB", "-z"]
    for off, name in images:
        cmd += [off, os.path.join(d, name)]
    try:
        rc = subprocess.run(cmd).returncode
    finally:
        GPIO.output(BOOT, GPIO.HIGH)   # always release BOOT, even on failure
        time.sleep(0.05)

    print("[3/3] reset into run mode -- boot output:")
    with serial.Serial(PORT, 115200, timeout=0.1) as s:
        s.reset_input_buffer()
        GPIO.output(EN, GPIO.LOW); time.sleep(0.2); GPIO.output(EN, GPIO.HIGH)
        t0 = time.time()
        while time.time() - t0 < 2.5:
            line = s.readline().decode(errors="replace").rstrip()
            if line.startswith("BOOT"):
                print(f"  {line}")
    # No GPIO.cleanup(): leave EN/BOOT driven HIGH so the ESP32 isn't left floating.

    if was_running:
        subprocess.run(["sudo", "-n", "systemctl", "start", "pi-link"])
        print("pi-link restarted")
    print("\nFLASH OK" if rc == 0 else "\nFLASH FAILED (esptool error above)")
    sys.exit(rc)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: \Dual_Archi\tools\flow_test.py ===

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""flow_test.py -- measure the real flow rate through each valve.

For each pot: put a measuring cup under its emitter, press Enter, and the ESP32
runs the real watering path (valve opens -> 0.25 s -> pump ON for N seconds ->
pump OFF -> 0.25 s -> valve closes). Type how many mL you collected; it works
out mL/s, saves every run, and at the end compares the result with the
firmware's PUMP_FLOW_ML_PER_S (the number used to turn a dose in mL into pump
time).

  python3 flow_test.py                      all 9 pots, 5 s each, 1 run
  python3 flow_test.py --pots 1,4,7 --runs 3 --seconds 5

Results are appended to ~/thesis/flow_calibration.csv.

The ESP32 is NOT given the time here, so no automatic irrigation can start in
the middle of the test.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import os
import re
import statistics
import sys
from datetime import datetime

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from actuator_test import watched          # noqa: E402  (reboot detection)
from esp_link import EspLink, info_dict, stop_daemon  # noqa: E402

DUAL = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
OUT_CSV = os.path.join(DUAL, "flow_calibration.csv")
FW = os.path.join(DUAL, "Dual_Archi_Firmware", "src", "main.cpp")


def firmware_flow():
    try:
        m = re.search(r"PUMP_FLOW_ML_PER_S\s*=\s*([\d.]+)", open(FW).read())
        return float(m.group(1)) if m else None
    except OSError:
        return None


def ask_ml(prompt):
    """Returns float mL, None to skip, or 'redo'."""
    while True:
        a = input(prompt).strip().lower()
        if a == "":
            return None
        if a in ("r", "redo"):
            return "redo"
        try:
            v = float(a)
            if v >= 0:
                return v
        except ValueError:
            pass
        print("    type the mL collected (e.g. 96), r to redo, or Enter to skip")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Per-valve flow rate test")
    ap.add_argument("--pots", default="1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9", help="e.g. 1,4,7")
    ap.add_argument("--seconds", type=float, default=5.0, help="pump time per run (max 15)")
    ap.add_argument("--runs", type=int, default=1, help="runs per pot (3 gives a better average)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    pots = [int(p) for p in args.pots.split(",") if p.strip()]
    if not pots or not all(1 <= p <= 9 for p in pots):
        sys.exit("--pots must be numbers 1-9")
    ms = int(min(max(args.seconds, 0.5), 15.0) * 1000)

    stop_daemon()
    results = {p: [] for p in pots}
    new_file = not os.path.exists(OUT_CSV)
    with EspLink() as esp, open(OUT_CSV, "a", newline="") as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        if new_file:
            w.writerow(["timestamp", "pot_id", "pump_ms", "ml_collected", "ml_per_s"])
        info = info_dict(esp.ask("INFO", "INFO", 3))
        if info.get("actuators") != "ENABLED":
            sys.exit("ESP32 actuators are DISABLED -- set ACTUATORS_ENABLED = true and ./flash_esp.sh")

        print(f"\nFlow test: pots {pots}, pump {ms / 1000:g} s per run, {args.runs} run(s) each.")
        print("After each run type the mL collected, 'r' to redo, or Enter to skip.  Ctrl-C quits.\n")
        try:
            for pot in pots:
                run = 0
                while run < args.runs:
                    input(f"P-0{pot} run {run + 1}/{args.runs}: cup under P-0{pot}, then press Enter ")
                    ok = watched(esp, f"MANUAL_WATER,{pot - 1},{ms}", "ACK,MANUAL_WATER", ms / 1000 + 4)
                    if not ok:
                        print("    run failed -- not recorded")
                        if input("    try again? [Y/n] ").strip().lower() != "n":
                            continue
                        break
                    ml = ask_ml("    mL collected: ")
                    if ml == "redo":
                        continue
                    run += 1
                    if ml is None:
                        print("    skipped")
                        continue
                    rate = ml / (ms / 1000)
                    results[pot].append(rate)
                    w.writerow([datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
                                f"P-0{pot}", ms, ml, f"{rate:.2f}"])
                    f.flush()
                    print(f"    -> {rate:.1f} mL/s")
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print("\n  stopped early")

    rates = {p: statistics.mean(v) for p, v in results.items() if v}
    if not rates:
        print("no measurements recorded")
        return
    print("\n== RESULTS (mL/s)")
    for p, r in rates.items():
        spread = (f"  (runs: {', '.join(f'{x:.1f}' for x in results[p])})"
                  if len(results[p]) > 1 else "")
        print(f"  P-0{p}: {r:5.1f}{spread}")
    overall = statistics.mean(rates.values())
    print(f"  average: {overall:.1f} mL/s")
    if len(rates) > 1:
        lo, hi = min(rates.values()), max(rates.values())
        pct = (hi - lo) / overall * 100
        print(f"  spread between valves: {lo:.1f}-{hi:.1f} mL/s ({pct:.0f}%)")
        if pct > 15:
            print("  ! valves differ by more than 15% -- one flow number for all pots will\n"
                  "    over- or under-water some of them. Consider a per-pot flow table.")
    fw = firmware_flow()
    if fw:
        print(f"\n  firmware PUMP_FLOW_ML_PER_S = {fw:g}")
        if abs(overall - fw) / fw > 0.05:
            print(f"  -> update it to {overall:.1f} in Dual_Archi_Firmware/src/main.cpp, then ./flash_esp.sh")
            print(f"     (right now a 100 mL dose actually delivers about {100 * overall / fw:.0f} mL)")
        else:
            print("  -> within 5%, no change needed")
    print(f"\nsaved to {OUT_CSV}")
    print("pi-link is still stopped; start the system with tools/agrisentry.sh start")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: \Dual_Archi\tools\gpr_validate.py ===

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
gpr_validate.py -- prove the GPR mask is correct against human ground truth.

The adviser's question: "how do we know the GPR we get is correct?"  Answer:
people hand-paint the leaf pixels in each pot of saved noon frames WITHOUT
seeing the automatic mask, then this script compares the HSV mask against
those hand labels pixel by pixel.

    export   frames -> one self-contained annotate.html (open in any browser,
             paint leaves, press "Download labels")
    score    labels JSON(s) -> per-pot precision / recall / IoU / Dice,
             GPR(auto) vs GPR(manual), MAE, bias, Pearson r, Bland-Altman
             limits, and inter-annotator agreement when 2+ people labelled.

The automatic mask is recomputed exactly as capture_gpr.py does it (same HSV
band, same full-frame 3x3 opening, same circular ROI), so the numbers apply
to the dataset as logged.

USAGE (Mac or Pi; needs opencv + numpy)
    python3 tools/gpr_validate.py export FRAME.png [FRAME.png ...] --out val/
    python3 tools/gpr_validate.py score val/labels_*.json --frames-dir FRAMES/
    python3 tools/gpr_validate.py score ... --leaf-only   # leaf blades only

Frames live on the Pi in ~/agrisentry_data/frames/ (copied to the Mac by
./sync_pi.sh pull into _pi_data/agrisentry_data/frames/).
"""

import argparse
import base64
import csv
import json
import math
import os
import sys
from itertools import combinations

import cv2
import numpy as np

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
DUAL = os.path.dirname(HERE)
sys.path.insert(0, DUAL)
import capture_gpr as cg  # noqa: E402  (same mask code as the live pipeline)

ROI_FILE = os.path.join(DUAL, "pot_rois.json")
TEMPLATE = os.path.join(HERE, "gpr_annotate_template.html")


def load_rois(path):
    with open(path) as f:
        cfg = json.load(f)
    return cfg["rois"], cfg.get("circular", True)


def roi_circle_local(roi):
    """ROI circle inside its own w x h crop, as a 0/1 array."""
    cx, cy, r = cg.roi_geometry(roi)
    m = cg.circle_mask((roi["h"], roi["w"]), cx - roi["x"], cy - roi["y"], r)
    return m > 0


def crop(a, roi):
    return a[roi["y"]:roi["y"] + roi["h"], roi["x"]:roi["x"] + roi["w"]]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- export
def cmd_export(args):
    rois, _ = load_rois(args.roi_file)
    items = []
    for path in args.frames:
        img = cv2.imread(path)
        if img is None:
            sys.exit(f"cannot read {path}")
        for pid in sorted(rois):
            roi = rois[pid]
            ok, png = cv2.imencode(".png", crop(img, roi))
            cx, cy, r = cg.roi_geometry(roi)
            items.append({
                "frame": os.path.basename(path), "pot": pid,
                "w": roi["w"], "h": roi["h"],
                "cx": cx - roi["x"], "cy": cy - roi["y"], "r": r,
                "img": "data:image/png;base64," + base64.b64encode(png).decode(),
            })
    with open(TEMPLATE) as f:
        html = f.read().replace("/*__ITEMS__*/[]", json.dumps(items))
    os.makedirs(args.out, exist_ok=True)
    out = os.path.join(args.out, "annotate.html")
    with open(out, "w") as f:
        f.write(html)
    print(f"{len(items)} pot crops from {len(args.frames)} frame(s) -> {out}")
    print("Each annotator: open it, paint every leaf pixel, Download labels.")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- score
def rle_decode(rle, n):
    out = np.zeros(n, np.uint8)
    pos, val = 0, 0
    for run in rle:
        if val:
            out[pos:pos + run] = 1
        pos += run
        val ^= 1
    return out


def load_labels(paths):
    """{annotator: {(frame, pot): bool array}}"""
    by = {}
    for p in paths:
        with open(p) as f:
            d = json.load(f)
        who = d.get("annotator") or os.path.basename(p)
        lab = by.setdefault(who, {})
        for it in d["items"]:
            if not it.get("done"):
                continue
            m = rle_decode(it["rle"], it["w"] * it["h"]).reshape(it["h"], it["w"])
            lab[(it["frame"], it["pot"])] = m.astype(bool)
    return by


def seg_metrics(auto, man):
    tp = int(np.sum(auto & man))
    fp = int(np.sum(auto & ~man))
    fn = int(np.sum(~auto & man))
    prec = tp / (tp + fp) if tp + fp else float("nan")
    rec = tp / (tp + fn) if tp + fn else float("nan")
    iou = tp / (tp + fp + fn) if tp + fp + fn else float("nan")
    dice = 2 * tp / (2 * tp + fp + fn) if tp + fp + fn else float("nan")
    return tp, fp, fn, prec, rec, iou, dice


def pearson(x, y):
    if len(x) < 3:
        return float("nan")
    return float(np.corrcoef(x, y)[0, 1])


def cmd_score(args):
    rois, _ = load_rois(args.roi_file)
    raw_labels = load_labels(args.labels)
    if not raw_labels:
        sys.exit("no finished labels in those files")
    # Clip to the ROI circle, and in --leaf-only mode drop 1-2 px wide stems
    # (GPR measures leaf cover). Done once here so the program-vs-human AND the
    # human-vs-human comparisons use the same definition of "leaf".
    labels = {}
    for who, lab in raw_labels.items():
        labels[who] = {}
        for key, man in lab.items():
            man = man & roi_circle_local(rois[key[1]])
            if args.leaf_only:
                man = cv2.morphologyEx(man.astype(np.uint8), cv2.MORPH_OPEN,
                                       np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8)) > 0
            labels[who][key] = man
    auto_cache = {}

    def auto_mask(frame):
        if frame not in auto_cache:
            img = cv2.imread(os.path.join(args.frames_dir, frame))
            if img is None:
                sys.exit(f"frame {frame} not found in {args.frames_dir}")
            hsv = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
            auto_cache[frame] = cg.green_mask(hsv) > 0   # full-frame opening
        return auto_cache[frame]

    rows = []
    for who, lab in sorted(labels.items()):
        for (frame, pot), man in sorted(lab.items()):
            roi = rois[pot]
            circ = roi_circle_local(roi)
            auto = crop(auto_mask(frame), roi) & circ
            area = int(circ.sum())
            tp, fp, fn, prec, rec, iou, dice = seg_metrics(auto, man)
            rows.append({
                "annotator": who, "frame": frame, "pot": pot,
                "roi_px": area, "auto_px": int(auto.sum()), "manual_px": int(man.sum()),
                "gpr_auto": 100 * auto.sum() / area, "gpr_manual": 100 * man.sum() / area,
                "tp": tp, "fp": fp, "fn": fn,
                "precision": prec, "recall": rec, "iou": iou, "dice": dice,
            })

    out_csv = args.out
    with open(out_csv, "w", newline="") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(rows[0]))
        w.writeheader()
        for r in rows:
            w.writerow({k: (f"{v:.4f}" if isinstance(v, float) else v) for k, v in r.items()})

    def summary(rs, title):
        ga = np.array([r["gpr_auto"] for r in rs])
        gm = np.array([r["gpr_manual"] for r in rs])
        d = ga - gm
        tp = sum(r["tp"] for r in rs); fp = sum(r["fp"] for r in rs); fn = sum(r["fn"] for r in rs)
        sd = float(np.std(d, ddof=1)) if len(d) > 1 else float("nan")
        print(f"\n{title}  (n = {len(rs)} pot-images)")
        print(f"  pixel precision {tp/(tp+fp):.3f}   recall {tp/(tp+fn):.3f}   "
              f"IoU {tp/(tp+fp+fn):.3f}   Dice {2*tp/(2*tp+fp+fn):.3f}   (pooled)")
        print(f"  mean per-pot IoU {np.nanmean([r['iou'] for r in rs]):.3f}")
        print(f"  GPR auto vs manual: MAE {np.mean(np.abs(d)):.3f} pp   bias {np.mean(d):+.3f} pp   "
              f"Pearson r {pearson(ga, gm):.3f}")
        print(f"  Bland-Altman 95% limits: {np.mean(d) - 1.96*sd:+.3f} to {np.mean(d) + 1.96*sd:+.3f} pp")
        rel = np.abs(d) / np.where(gm > 0, gm, np.nan)
        print(f"  mean relative GPR error {100*np.nanmean(rel):.1f} %")

    print("GPR VALIDATION -- automatic HSV mask vs hand-labelled "
          + ("leaf blades (thin stems removed from the labels)" if args.leaf_only else "plants"))
    summary(rows, "ALL ANNOTATORS")
    if len(labels) > 1:
        for who in sorted(labels):
            summary([r for r in rows if r["annotator"] == who], f"annotator: {who}")
        print("\nINTER-ANNOTATOR AGREEMENT (how consistent the human ground truth is)")
        for a, b in combinations(sorted(labels), 2):
            common = sorted(set(labels[a]) & set(labels[b]))
            ious, dg = [], []
            for key in common:
                circ = roi_circle_local(rois[key[1]])
                ma, mb = labels[a][key], labels[b][key]
                ious.append(seg_metrics(ma, mb)[5])
                dg.append(100 * abs(int(ma.sum()) - int(mb.sum())) / circ.sum())
            if common:
                print(f"  {a} vs {b}: n={len(common)}  mean IoU {np.nanmean(ious):.3f}  "
                      f"mean |dGPR| {np.mean(dg):.3f} pp")
                # Independence check: one person editing another's saved labels
                # leaves a near-perfect subset and pixel-identical images.
                R = raw_labels   # as drawn, before any stem removal
                same = sum(bool((R[a][k] == R[b][k]).all()) for k in common)
                for x, y in ((a, b), (b, a)):
                    px = sum(int(R[x][k].sum()) for k in common)
                    out = sum(int((R[x][k] & ~R[y][k]).sum()) for k in common)
                    if px and out / px < 0.02:
                        print(f"  WARNING: {100 * (1 - out / px):.1f}% of {x}'s pixels lie inside "
                              f"{y}'s labels ({same} image(s) identical) -- {x} probably "
                              f"edited {y}'s labels; this pair is NOT independent")
    print(f"\nper-pot table -> {out_csv}")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.split("\n")[1])
    ap.add_argument("--roi-file", default=ROI_FILE)
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    e = sub.add_parser("export")
    e.add_argument("frames", nargs="+")
    e.add_argument("--out", default="gpr_validation")
    s = sub.add_parser("score")
    s.add_argument("labels", nargs="+")
    s.add_argument("--frames-dir", required=True)
    s.add_argument("--out", default="gpr_validation.csv")
    s.add_argument("--leaf-only", action="store_true",
                   help="remove 1-2 px wide stems from the hand labels (3x3 opening, "
                        "the same kernel the pipeline uses) before scoring")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    {"export": cmd_export, "score": cmd_score}[args.cmd](args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: \Dual_Archi\tools\gpr_view.py ===

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""gpr_view.py -- SEE what the GPR algorithm measures.

The noon frame itself is never stored (storage autonomy), so this takes a fresh
frame with the exact same locked camera settings, runs the exact same mask
(capture_gpr.green_mask, same ROIs), and draws:

  top     the whole bed: counted plant pixels in MAGENTA, speckle removed by the
          3x3 opening in YELLOW, pot circles in cyan with today's GPR
  bottom  every pot close-up, laid out like the bed: photo | what was counted

Writes NO data. Usually run from the Mac with ./gpr_view.sh (photo lands on the
Mac desktop and opens).
